{"lang_code":"en","pos":"prep_phrase","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"We don't talk like that around here.","type":"example"},{"text":"I can't believe anyone past the age of six would behave like that in public.","type":"example"},{"text":"It happened just like that.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used adverbially or adjectivally to indicate similitude, similarity, equivalent or comparable quality","In such, the same, a similar way, in that particular manner"],"synonyms":[{"word":"like so"},{"word":"that way"}],"tags":["adverbial"]},{"examples":[{"text":"We don't say stuff like that around here.","type":"example"},{"text":"I've never seen anything like that in my life. Folks like that I just can’t stand.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used adverbially or adjectivally to indicate similitude, similarity, equivalent or comparable quality","Of the same, a similar, that particular kind."],"synonyms":[{"word":"such"},{"word":"of the/that kind"},{"word":"of the/that sort"},{"word":"of the like"},{"word":"of that ilk."}],"tags":["adjectival"]},{"glosses":["Used adverbially or adjectivally to indicate similitude, similarity, equivalent or comparable quality","Used at the end of a sentence to emphasize a resemblance to something, reinforcing a previous instance of the preposition like (or a similar term)."],"tags":["Singapore","Singlish"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He was playing in the yard and, then, like that, he was gone.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["suddenly; unexpectedly."],"synonyms":[{"word":"just like that"},{"word":"bam, wham"},{"word":"all of a sudden"},{"word":"out of nowhere"}],"tags":["adverbial"]},{"examples":[{"text":"\"And then the truck turned, the box fell out the back, and the truck just kept going.\" / \"Yeah, like that.\"","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used to indicate agreement with another speaker's statement."],"synonyms":[{"word":"what he said"}]}],"sounds":[],"upstream_line":2615896,"upstream_offset":7787041919,"upstream_record_sha256":"0fe51001d2cbb47f6b94564ccb86accf4aaa3119d2c595a72e12bd9dac113feb","word":"like that"}
